High weight on tensions, lower on velocities\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6\n        if R_diag is None:\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q_diag = np.array(Q_diag)\n        self.R_diag = np.array(R_diag)\n        self.Q = np.diag(self.Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(self.R_diag)\n\n        # Get initial reference for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n\n        # Linearize around initial operating point\n        self.A, self.B = self._linearize_dynamics(\n            self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial\n        )\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _linearize_dynamics(self, T_bar, v_bar):\n        \"\"\"\n        Linearize dynamics around operating point (T_bar, v_bar).\n\n        State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n        Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6]\n\n        Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        Returns:\n            A: 12x12 state matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n        \"\"\"\n        n = self.num_sec\n        A = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n        B = np.zeros((2*n, n))\n\n        # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Compute state deviation\n        x_dev = x_current - x_ref\n\n        # Build reference trajectory (assume constant for simplicity)\n        x_ref_seq = np.tile(x_ref, (self.horizon_N + 1, 1))\n        u_ref_seq = np.tile(u_ref, (self.horizon_N, 1))\n\n        # Solve finite-horizon LQR\n        u_opt_seq = self._solve_finite_horizon_lqr(x_dev, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq)\n\n        # Return first control + reference\n        u = u_opt_seq[0] + u_ref\n\n        return u\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        \"\"\"Return controller parameters as dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.horizon_N),\n            \"Q_diag\": self.Q_diag.tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": self.R_diag.tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(controller, sim, duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC control simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        controller: MPCController instance\n        sim: R2RSimulator instance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: List of timestep dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # 2N tolerance\n    settled = np.all(np.abs(tensions[step_idx:] - T_ref_final) < settling_threshold, axis=1)\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        settling_idx = step_idx + np.argmax(settled)\n        settling_time = float(times[settling_idx] - step_time)\n    else:\n        settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    # Max and min tensions over entire simulation\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run MPC controller.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Load system parameters\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.EA = float(config[\"EA\"])\n        self.J = float(config[\"J\"])\n        self.R = float(config[\"R\"])\n        self.fb = float(config[\"fb\"])\n        self.L = float(config[\"L\"])\n        self.v0 = float(config[\"v0\"])\n        self.dt = float(config[\"dt\"])\n        self.num_sec = int(config[\"num_sections\"])\n\n        # Debug: verify types\n        assert isinstance(self.R, (int, float)), f\"R is {type(self.R)}\"\n        assert isinstance(self.J, (int, float)), f\"J is {type(self.J)}\"\n\n        # Set default weights if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            # High weight on tensions, lower on velocities\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6\n        if R_diag is None:\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q_diag = np.array(Q_diag)\n        self.R_diag = np.array(R_diag)\n        self.Q = np.diag(self.Q_diag)\n        self.R = np.diag(self.R_diag)\n\n        # Get initial reference for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n\n        # Linearize around initial operating point\n        self.A, self.B = self._linearize_dynamics(\n            self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial\n        )\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _linearize_dynamics(self, T_bar, v_bar):\n        \"\"\"\n        Linearize dynamics around operating point (T_bar, v_bar).\n\n        State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n        Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6]\n\n        Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        Returns:\n            A: 12x12 state matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n        \"\"\"\n        n = self.num_sec\n        A = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n        B = np.zeros((2*n, n))\n\n        # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        print(f\"DEBUG: c1={c1}, type={type(c1)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.R={self.R}, type={type(self.R)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.J={self.J}, type={type(self.J)}\")\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Compute state deviation\n        x_dev = x_current - x_ref\n\n        # Build reference trajectory (assume constant for simplicity)\n        x_ref_seq = np.tile(x_ref, (self.horizon_N + 1, 1))\n        u_ref_seq = np.tile(u_ref, (self.horizon_N, 1))\n\n        # Solve finite-horizon LQR\n        u_opt_seq = self._solve_finite_horizon_lqr(x_dev, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq)\n\n        # Return first control + reference\n        u = u_opt_seq[0] + u_ref\n\n        return u\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        \"\"\"Return controller parameters as dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.horizon_N),\n            \"Q_diag\": self.Q_diag.tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": self.R_diag.tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(controller, sim, duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC control simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        controller: MPCController instance\n        sim: R2RSimulator instance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: List of timestep dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    t = sim.get_time()\n\n    num_steps = int(duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(t),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # Find index after step\n    step_idx = np.argmax(times >= step_time)\n\n    # Steady-state error (average error in last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.argmax(times >= (times[-1] - 1.0))\n    steady_tensions = tensions[steady_start_idx:]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(T_ref_final)\n\n    # Mean absolute error across all sections\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - T_ref_final)))\n\n    # Settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of reference)\n    settling_threshold = 2.0  # 2N tolerance\n    settled = np.all(np.abs(tensions[step_idx:] - T_ref_final) < settling_threshold, axis=1)\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        settling_idx = step_idx + np.argmax(settled)\n        settling_time = float(times[settling_idx] - step_time)\n    else:\n        settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    # Max and min tensions over entire simulation\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run MPC controller.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Load configuration\n    with open(\"/root/system_config.json\", 'r') as f:\n        config = json.load(f)\n\n    # MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 9\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6  # High weight on tensions\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6  # Moderate control effort\n\n    print(f\"\\nMPC Parameters:\")\n    print(f\"  Horizon N: {horizon_N}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (tensions): {Q_diag[:6]}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (velocities): {Q_diag[6:]}\")\n    print(f\"  R_diag: {R_diag}\")\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        print(f\"DEBUG: c1={c1}, type={type(c1)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.R={self.R}, type={type(self.R)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.J={self.J}, type={type(self.J)}\")\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # Max and min tensions over entire simulation\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run MPC controller.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Load system parameters\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.EA = float(config[\"EA\"])\n        self.J = float(config[\"J\"])\n        self.R = float(config[\"R\"])\n        self.fb = float(config[\"fb\"])\n        self.L = float(config[\"L\"])\n        self.v0 = float(config[\"v0\"])\n        self.dt = float(config[\"dt\"])\n        self.num_sec = int(config[\"num_sections\"])\n\n        # Debug: verify types\n        assert isinstance(self.R, (int, float)), f\"R is {type(self.R)}\"\n        assert isinstance(self.J, (int, float)), f\"J is {type(self.J)}\"\n\n        # Set default weights if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            # High weight on tensions, lower on velocities\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6\n        if R_diag is None:\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q_cost_diag = np.array(Q_diag)\n        self.R_diag = np.array(R_diag)\n        self.Q_cost_cost = np.diag(self.Q_diag)\n        self.R_cost = np.diag(self.R_diag)\n\n        # Get initial reference for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n\n        # Linearize around initial operating point\n        self.A, self.B = self._linearize_dynamics(\n            self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial\n        )\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _linearize_dynamics(self, T_bar, v_bar):\n        \"\"\"\n        Linearize dynamics around operating point (T_bar, v_bar).\n\n        State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n        Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6]\n\n        Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        Returns:\n            A: 12x12 state matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n        \"\"\"\n        n = self.num_sec\n        A = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n        B = np.zeros((2*n, n))\n\n        # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        print(f\"DEBUG: c1={c1}, type={type(c1)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.R={self.R}, type={type(self.R)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.J={self.J}, type={type(self.J)}\")\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Compute state deviation\n        x_dev = x_current - x_ref\n\n        # Build reference trajectory (assume constant for simplicity)\n        x_ref_seq = np.tile(x_ref, (self.horizon_N + 1, 1))\n        u_ref_seq = np.tile(u_ref, (self.horizon_N, 1))\n\n        # Solve finite-horizon LQR\n        u_opt_seq = self._solve_finite_horizon_lqr(x_dev, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq)\n\n        # Return first control + reference\n        u = u_opt_seq[0] + u_ref\n\n        return u\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        \"\"\"Return controller parameters as dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.horizon_N),\n            \"Q_diag\": self.Q_diag.tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": self.R_diag.tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(controller, sim, duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC control simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        controller: MPCController instance\n        sim: R2RSimulator instance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: List of timestep dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    t = sim.get_time()\n\n    num_steps = int(duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(t),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # Find index after step\n    step_idx = np.argmax(times >= step_time)\n\n    # Steady-state error (average error in last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.argmax(times >= (times[-1] - 1.0))\n    steady_tensions = tensions[steady_start_idx:]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(T_ref_final)\n\n    # Mean absolute error across all sections\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - T_ref_final)))\n\n    # Settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of reference)\n    settling_threshold = 2.0  # 2N tolerance\n    settled = np.all(np.abs(tensions[step_idx:] - T_ref_final) < settling_threshold, axis=1)\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        settling_idx = step_idx + np.argmax(settled)\n        settling_time = float(times[settling_idx] - step_time)\n    else:\n        settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    # Max and min tensions over entire simulation\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run MPC controller.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Load configuration\n    with open(\"/root/system_config.json\", 'r') as f:\n        config = json.load(f)\n\n    # MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 9\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6  # High weight on tensions\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6  # Moderate control effort\n\n    print(f\"\\nMPC Parameters:\")\n    print(f\"  Horizon N: {horizon_N}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (tensions): {Q_diag[:6]}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (velocities): {Q_diag[6:]}\")\n    print(f\"  R_diag: {R_diag}\")\n\n    # Load system parameters\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.EA = float(config[\"EA\"])\n        self.J = float(config[\"J\"])\n        self.R = float(config[\"R\"])\n        self.fb = float(config[\"fb\"])\n        self.L = float(config[\"L\"])\n        self.v0 = float(config[\"v0\"])\n        self.dt = float(config[\"dt\"])\n        self.num_sec = int(config[\"num_sections\"])\n\n        # Debug: verify types\n        assert isinstance(self.R, (int, float)), f\"R is {type(self.R)}\"\n        assert isinstance(self.J, (int, float)), f\"J is {type(self.J)}\"\n\n        # Set default weights if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            # High weight on tensions, lower on velocities\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6\n        if R_diag is None:\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q_cost_diag = np.array(Q_diag)\n        self.R_diag = np.array(R_diag)\n        self.Q_cost_cost = np.diag(self.Q_diag)\n        self.R_cost = np.diag(self.R_diag)\n\n        # Get initial reference for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n\n        # Linearize around initial operating point\n        self.A, self.B = self._linearize_dynamics(\n            self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial\n        )\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _linearize_dynamics(self, T_bar, v_bar):\n        \"\"\"\n        Linearize dynamics around operating point (T_bar, v_bar).\n\n        State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n        Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6]\n\n        Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        Returns:\n            A: 12x12 state matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n        \"\"\"\n        n = self.num_sec\n        A = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n        B = np.zeros((2*n, n))\n\n        # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        print(f\"DEBUG: c1={c1}, type={type(c1)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.R={self.R}, type={type(self.R)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.J={self.J}, type={type(self.J)}\")\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q_cost, self.R_cost)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R_cost + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Compute state deviation\n        x_dev = x_current - x_ref\n\n        # Build reference trajectory (assume constant for simplicity)\n        x_ref_seq = np.tile(x_ref, (self.horizon_N + 1, 1))\n        u_ref_seq = np.tile(u_ref, (self.horizon_N, 1))\n\n        # Solve finite-horizon LQR\n        u_opt_seq = self._solve_finite_horizon_lqr(x_dev, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq)\n\n        # Return first control + reference\n        u = u_opt_seq[0] + u_ref\n\n        return u\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        \"\"\"Return controller parameters as dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.horizon_N),\n            \"Q_diag\": self.Q_diag.tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": self.R_diag.tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(controller, sim, duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC control simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        controller: MPCController instance\n        sim: R2RSimulator instance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: List of timestep dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    t = sim.get_time()\n\n    num_steps = int(duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(t),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # Find index after step\n    step_idx = np.argmax(times >= step_time)\n\n    # Steady-state error (average error in last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.argmax(times >= (times[-1] - 1.0))\n    steady_tensions = tensions[steady_start_idx:]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(T_ref_final)\n\n    # Mean absolute error across all sections\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - T_ref_final)))\n\n    # Settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of reference)\n    settling_threshold = 2.0  # Load system parameters\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.EA = float(config[\"EA\"])\n        self.J = float(config[\"J\"])\n        self.R = float(config[\"R\"])\n        self.fb = float(config[\"fb\"])\n        self.L = float(config[\"L\"])\n        self.v0 = float(config[\"v0\"])\n        self.dt = float(config[\"dt\"])\n        self.num_sec = int(config[\"num_sections\"])\n\n        # Debug: verify types\n        assert isinstance(self.R, (int, float)), f\"R is {type(self.R)}\"\n        assert isinstance(self.J, (int, float)), f\"J is {type(self.J)}\"\n\n        # Set default weights if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            # High weight on tensions, lower on velocities\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6\n        if R_diag is None:\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q_cost_diag = np.array(Q_diag)\n        self.R_diag = np.array(R_diag)\n        self.Q_cost_cost = np.diag(self.Q_diag)\n        self.R_cost = np.diag(self.R_diag)\n\n        # Get initial reference for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n\n        # Linearize around initial operating point\n        self.A, self.B = self._linearize_dynamics(\n            self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial\n        )\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _linearize_dynamics(self, T_bar, v_bar):\n        \"\"\"\n        Linearize dynamics around operating point (T_bar, v_bar).\n\n        State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n        Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6]\n\n        Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        Returns:\n            A: 12x12 state matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n        \"\"\"\n        n = self.num_sec\n        A = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n        B = np.zeros((2*n, n))\n\n        # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        print(f\"DEBUG: c1={c1}, type={type(c1)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.R={self.R}, type={type(self.R)}\")\n        print(f\"DEBUG: self.J={self.J}, type={type(self.J)}\")\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q_cost, self.R_cost)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R_cost + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q_cost, self.R_cost)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R_cost + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q_cost + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Compute state deviation\n        x_dev = x_current - x_ref\n\n        # Build reference trajectory (assume constant for simplicity)\n        x_ref_seq = np.tile(x_ref, (self.horizon_N + 1, 1))\n        u_ref_seq = np.tile(u_ref, (self.horizon_N, 1))\n\n        # Solve finite-horizon LQR\n        u_opt_seq = self._solve_finite_horizon_lqr(x_dev, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq)\n\n        # Return first control + reference\n        u = u_opt_seq[0] + u_ref\n\n        return u\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        \"\"\"Return controller parameters as dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.horizon_N),\n            \"Q_diag\": self.Q_diag.tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": self.R_diag.tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(controller, sim, duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC control simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        controller: MPCController instance\n        sim: R2RSimulator instance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: List of timestep dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    t = sim.get_time()\n\n    num_steps = int(duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(t),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # Find index after step\n    step_idx = np.argmax(times >= step_time)\n\n    # Steady-state error (average error in last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.argmax(times >= (times[-1] - 1.0))\n    steady_tensions = tensions[steady_start_idx:]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(T_ref_final)\n\n    # Mean absolute error across all sections\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - T_ref_final)))\n\n    # Settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of reference)\n    settling_threshold = 2.0  # 2N tolerance\n    settled = np.all(np.abs(tensions[step_idx:] - T_ref_final) < settling_threshold, axis=1)\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        settling_idx = step_idx + np.argmax(settled)\n        settling_time = float(times[settling_idx] - step_time)\n    else:\n        settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    # Max and min tensions over entire simulation\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run MPC controller.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Load configuration\n    with open(\"/root/system_config.json\", 'r') as f:\n        config = json.load(f)\n\n    # MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 9\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6  # High weight on tensions\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6  # Moderate control effort\n\n    print(f\"\\nMPC Parameters:\")\n    print(f\"  Horizon N: {horizon_N}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (tensions): {Q_diag[:6]}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (velocities): {Q_diag[6:]}\")\n    print(f\"  R_diag: {R_diag}\")\n\n    # Set default weights if not provided","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\n\nImplements Model Predictive Control with:\n- State-space linearization around operating points\n- Finite-horizon LQR for optimal control\n- Reference tracking with step changes\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"MPC controller for 6-section R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/system_config.json\",\n                 horizon_N=9, Q_diag=None, R_diag=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize MPC controller.\n\n        Args:\n            config_path: Path to system configuration\n            horizon_N: Prediction horizon (3-30)\n            Q_diag: State cost weights (12 elements)\n            R_diag: Control cost weights (6 elements)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.config_path = config_path\n        self.horizon_N = horizon_N\n\n        # Get initial reference for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n\n        # Linearize around initial operating point\n        self.A, self.B = self._linearize_dynamics(\n            self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial\n        )\n\n        # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q_cost, self.R_cost)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R_cost + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q_cost, self.R_cost)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R_cost + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q_cost + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Compute state deviation\n        x_dev = x_current - x_ref\n\n        # Build reference trajectory (assume constant for simplicity)\n        x_ref_seq = np.tile(x_ref, (self.horizon_N + 1, 1))\n        u_ref_seq = np.tile(u_ref, (self.horizon_N, 1))\n\n        # Solve finite-horizon LQR\n        u_opt_seq = self._solve_finite_horizon_lqr(x_dev, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq)\n\n        # Return first control + reference\n        u = u_opt_seq[0] + u_ref\n\n        return u\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        \"\"\"Return controller parameters as dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.horizon_N),\n            \"Q_diag\": self.Q_diag.tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": self.R_diag.tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(controller, sim, duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC control simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        controller: MPCController instance\n        sim: R2RSimulator instance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: List of timestep dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    t = sim.get_time()\n\n    num_steps = int(duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(t),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # Find index after step\n    step_idx = np.argmax(times >= step_time)\n\n    # Steady-state error (average error in last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.argmax(times >= (times[-1] - 1.0))\n    steady_tensions = tensions[steady_start_idx:]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(T_ref_final)\n\n    # Mean absolute error across all sections\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - T_ref_final)))\n\n    # Settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of reference)\n    settling_threshold = 2.0  # 2N tolerance\n    settled = np.all(np.abs(tensions[step_idx:] - T_ref_final) < settling_threshold, axis=1)\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        settling_idx = step_idx + np.argmax(settled)\n        settling_time = float(times[settling_idx] - step_time)\n    else:\n        settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    # Max and min tensions over entire simulation\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run MPC controller.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Load system parameters\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.EA = float(config[\"EA\"])\n        self.J = float(config[\"J\"])\n        self.R = float(config[\"R\"])\n        self.fb = float(config[\"fb\"])\n        self.L = float(config[\"L\"])\n        self.v0 = float(config[\"v0\"])\n        self.dt = float(config[\"dt\"])\n        self.num_sec = int(config[\"num_sections\"])\n\n        # Set default weights if not provided\n        if Q_diag is None:\n            # High weight on tensions, lower on velocities\n            Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6\n        if R_diag is None:\n            # Moderate control effort penalty\n            R_diag = [0.033] * 6\n\n        self.Q_cost_diag = np.array(Q_diag)\n        self.R_diag = np.array(R_diag)\n        self.Q_cost_cost = np.diag(self.Q_diag)\n        self.R_cost = np.diag(self.R_diag)\n\n        # Get initial reference for linearization\n        self.T_ref_initial = np.array(config[\"T_ref_initial\"])\n        self.v_ref_initial = self._compute_velocities(self.T_ref_initial)\n\n        # Linearize around initial operating point\n        self.A, self.B = self._linearize_dynamics(\n            self.T_ref_initial, self.v_ref_initial\n        )\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        self.Ad, self.Bd = self._discretize(self.A, self.B, self.dt)\n\n        # Compute LQR gain\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n    def _compute_velocities(self, T_ref):\n        \"\"\"Compute steady-state velocities for given tension profile.\"\"\"\n        v_ref = np.zeros(self.num_sec)\n        v_p, T_p = self.v0, 0.0\n        for i in range(self.num_sec):\n            v_ref[i] = (self.EA - T_p) / (self.EA - T_ref[i]) * v_p\n            v_p, T_p = v_ref[i], T_ref[i]\n        return v_ref\n\n    def _linearize_dynamics(self, T_bar, v_bar):\n        \"\"\"\n        Linearize dynamics around operating point (T_bar, v_bar).\n\n        State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n        Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6]\n\n        Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        Returns:\n            A: 12x12 state matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n        \"\"\"\n        n = self.num_sec\n        A = np.zeros((2*n, 2*n))\n        B = np.zeros((2*n, n))\n\n        # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dT_i/dT_{i-1}: (1/L)*v_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, i-1] = float((1/self.L) * v_bar[i-1])\n\n            # dT_i/dT_i: -(1/L)*v_i\n            A[i, i] = float(-(1/self.L) * v_bar[i])\n\n            # dT_i/dv_{i-1}: (EA/L) + (1/L)*T_{i-1}\n            if i > 0:\n                A[i, n+i-1] = float((self.EA/self.L) + (1/self.L) * T_bar[i-1])\n            else:\n                # For i=0, v_{i-1} = v0 (constant), no coupling\n                pass\n\n            # dT_i/dv_i: -(EA/L) - (1/L)*T_i\n            A[i, n+i] = float(-(self.EA/self.L) - (1/self.L) * T_bar[i])\n\n        # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n        # Pre-compute constants\n        c1 = self.R**2 / self.J\n        c2 = self.fb / self.J\n        c3 = self.R / self.J\n\n        for i in range(n):\n            # dv_i/dT_i: -(R^2/J)\n            A[n+i, i] = -c1\n\n            # dv_i/dT_{i+1}: (R^2/J)\n            if i < n-1:\n                A[n+i, i+1] = c1\n\n            # dv_i/dv_i: -(fb/J)\n            A[n+i, n+i] = -c2\n\n            # dv_i/du_i: (R/J)\n            B[n+i, i] = c3\n\n        return A, B\n\n    def _discretize(self, A, B, dt):\n        \"\"\"Discretize continuous-time system using matrix exponential.\"\"\"\n        n = A.shape[0]\n        m = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Build augmented matrix\n        M = np.zeros((n+m, n+m))\n        M[:n, :n] = A * dt\n        M[:n, n:] = B * dt\n\n        # Matrix exponential\n        EM = linalg.expm(M)\n\n        Ad = EM[:n, :n]\n        Bd = EM[:n, n:]\n\n        return Ad, Bd\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute LQR feedback gain for infinite-horizon problem.\n        Used as terminal cost for MPC.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q_cost, self.R_cost)\n\n        # Compute feedback gain\n        K = linalg.inv(self.R_cost + self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Bd) @ (self.Bd.T @ P @ self.Ad)\n\n        return K\n\n    def _solve_finite_horizon_lqr(self, x0, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve finite-horizon LQR via dynamic programming.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state deviation from reference\n            x_ref_seq: Reference state trajectory (N+1 x 12)\n            u_ref_seq: Reference control trajectory (N x 6)\n\n        Returns:\n            u_opt: Optimal control sequence (N x 6)\n        \"\"\"\n        N = self.horizon_N\n        n = self.Ad.shape[0]\n        m = self.Bd.shape[1]\n\n        # Terminal cost from infinite-horizon LQR\n        P_inf = linalg.solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q_cost, self.R_cost)\n\n        # Backward pass: compute value function and gains\n        P = [None] * (N + 1)\n        K = [None] * N\n\n        P[N] = P_inf\n\n        for k in range(N-1, -1, -1):\n            # Riccati recursion\n            temp = linalg.inv(self.R_cost + self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd)\n            K[k] = temp @ (self.Bd.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad)\n            P[k] = self.Q_cost + self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Ad - \\\n                   self.Ad.T @ P[k+1] @ self.Bd @ K[k]\n\n        # Forward pass: compute optimal control sequence\n        u_opt = np.zeros((N, m))\n        x = x0.copy()\n\n        for k in range(N):\n            u_opt[k] = -K[k] @ x\n            x = self.Ad @ x + self.Bd @ u_opt[k]\n\n        return u_opt\n\n    def compute_control(self, x_current, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control action.\n\n        Args:\n            x_current: Current state (12,)\n            x_ref: Current reference state (12,)\n            u_ref: Current reference control (6,)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input (6,)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Compute state deviation\n        x_dev = x_current - x_ref\n\n        # Build reference trajectory (assume constant for simplicity)\n        x_ref_seq = np.tile(x_ref, (self.horizon_N + 1, 1))\n        u_ref_seq = np.tile(u_ref, (self.horizon_N, 1))\n\n        # Solve finite-horizon LQR\n        u_opt_seq = self._solve_finite_horizon_lqr(x_dev, x_ref_seq, u_ref_seq)\n\n        # Return first control + reference\n        u = u_opt_seq[0] + u_ref\n\n        return u\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        \"\"\"Return controller parameters as dictionary.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"horizon_N\": int(self.horizon_N),\n            \"Q_diag\": self.Q_diag.tolist(),\n            \"R_diag\": self.R_diag.tolist(),\n            \"K_lqr\": self.K_lqr.tolist(),\n            \"A_matrix\": self.A.tolist(),\n            \"B_matrix\": self.B.tolist()\n        }\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(controller, sim, duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC control simulation.\n\n    Args:\n        controller: MPCController instance\n        sim: R2RSimulator instance\n        duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: List of timestep dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    log_data = []\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n    t = sim.get_time()\n\n    num_steps = int(duration / sim.dt)\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": float(t),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n        # Step simulator\n        x = sim.step(u)\n        t = sim.get_time()\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, T_ref_initial, T_ref_final, step_time):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged timestep dictionaries\n        T_ref_initial: Initial reference tensions\n        T_ref_final: Final reference tensions\n        step_time: Time of reference step change\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in log_data])\n    tensions = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in log_data])\n\n    # Find index after step\n    step_idx = np.argmax(times >= step_time)\n\n    # Steady-state error (average error in last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.argmax(times >= (times[-1] - 1.0))\n    steady_tensions = tensions[steady_start_idx:]\n    T_ref_final = np.array(T_ref_final)\n\n    # Mean absolute error across all sections\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - T_ref_final)))\n\n    # Settling time (when all tensions stay within 2% of reference)\n    settling_threshold = 2.0  # 2N tolerance\n    settled = np.all(np.abs(tensions[step_idx:] - T_ref_final) < settling_threshold, axis=1)\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        settling_idx = step_idx + np.argmax(settled)\n        settling_time = float(times[settling_idx] - step_time)\n    else:\n        settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    # Max and min tensions over entire simulation\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": steady_state_error,\n        \"settling_time\": settling_time,\n        \"max_tension\": max_tension,\n        \"min_tension\": min_tension\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to run MPC controller.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Load configuration\n    with open(\"/root/system_config.json\", 'r') as f:\n        config = json.load(f)\n\n    # MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 9\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6  # High weight on tensions\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6  # Moderate control effort\n\n    print(f\"\\nMPC Parameters:\")\n    print(f\"  Horizon N: {horizon_N}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (tensions): {Q_diag[:6]}\")\n    print(f\"  Q_diag (velocities): {Q_diag[6:]}\")\n    print(f\"  R_diag: {R_diag}\")\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    print(\"\\nInitializing MPC controller...\")\n    controller = MPCController(\n        config_path=\"/root/system_config.json\",\n        horizon_N=horizon_N,\n        Q_diag=Q_diag,\n        R_diag=R_diag\n    )\n    print(\"  Linearization complete\")\n    print(\"  LQR gain computed\")\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    params_dict = controller.get_params_dict()\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(params_dict, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"  Saved controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Initialize simulator\n    print(\"\\nInitializing simulator...\")\n    sim = R2RSimulator(\"/root/system_config.json\")\n    print(f\"  System: {config['num_sections']} sections\")\n    print(f\"  Step change: T3 = {config['T_ref_initial'][2]}N -> {config['T_ref_final'][2]}N at t={config['step_time']}s\")\n\n    #
